## Whole Cake Island Arc (WCI 22 [865-866] — "Hey, Mother" / assassination aftermath (Bege/Katakuri/Perospero, mirror escape, Reiju thanks Sanji, Pedro) then Big Mom origin flashback: 5-year-old Linlin abandoned on Elbaf, Sister→Mother Carmel & the Sheep's House, Giant Warrior Pirates history, Winter Solstice fast & semla, Linlin's gluttony rampage)

DECISION for WCI 22: "Mother" honorific = 媽媽 throughout (children's address + Big Mom's cry + episode title "Hey, Mother" 嘿，媽媽). "Mother Carmel" full = 卡爾梅露媽媽 (matches WCI 20 L4057). Narrator "she became 'Mother' Carmel" = 她成了「媽媽」卡爾梅露. ## Whole Cake Island Arc (WCI 36 [892-895] — "Pirate Luffy vs. Commander Katakuri" / Katakuri vs Luffy climax: Gear Fourth Snakeman (Python/Culverin/Jet Culverin/Black Mamba/King Cobra) vs Katakuri's Grilled/Diced/Punch Mochi, Rayleigh training flashback (Observation Haki / seeing the future / "Rise above it, Luffy"), Shakky's "Monkey-chan" ; Bege's group flees with the cake — Chiffon insists on luring Hunger-Pang Big Mom (Prometheus) to Puffs Island not Liqueur Island so Nami's ship stays safe ; Sanji leaves Bege's ship for the Sunny via Pudding (mirror), Pudding's "Sanji-san" gag ; the Sunny pincered by Smoothie's fleet, Carrot (post-Sulong) & Jinbe dodge Smoothie's giant-body slams ; Oven's mirror-purge on Cacao Island, Charlotte siblings assemble (decuplets + Snack/Brownie/Joconde/Chiboust/Raisin/Yuen/Myukuru) awaiting the 1 AM Straw Hat rendezvous)

## Whole Cake Island Arc (WCI 37 [896-897] — "One Last Thing" / Cacao Island siblings (decuplets Newji/Newsan/Newshi + Akimeg) predict Straw Hat's 1 AM mirror-rendezvous & plot to capture Brûlée; Sanji's mirror farewell to Pudding (her tearful "Sanji-san" gag + "one last favour"); Morgans/Marine-scale fleet report; Vito & Bege's burning ship reaches Puffs Island; Nami/Chopper/Brook fret over Luffy escaping without Brûlée (Rayleigh/"fall forward" flashbacks); Luffy beats Katakuri, Brûlée ("Branch" gag) flees, Pekoms captures her & disguises as "Nazoms" the mysterious savior (Pedro's sacrifice); Sunny pincered between Snack's Totto-Land Grand Fleet & Smoothie's fleet, Mont d'Or links snails; Pekoms goes Sulong on Cacao, Oven's "Heat Wind Fist", Luffy bursts out; Moonwalk sniper squad; harbor explosion = Germa 66's fleet arrives)

## Whole Cake Island Arc (WCI 38 [898-900] — Germa 66's fleet crashes Cacao Island: Ichiji/Niji/Yonji's Raid-Suit attacks blast Big Mom Pirates while Sanji escapes with Luffy toward the harbour; Mont d'Or radios the shattered Germa-attack survivors (Nusstorte/Basskarte/Dosmarche) / Big-Mom-fleet radio net (Perospero fading from his wound, Bavarois, Mont d'Or) reveals the 3-outcome gamble on the poisoned wedding cake; Morgans & Stussy flee to leak the news / Sanji reaches the Sunny with Luffy, crew reunion; Oven's fleet & Daifuku's genie re-pincer the Sunny but Jinbe's old Sun-Pirates crew (Wadatsumi) sink the paddle-ships & clear a path / Sanji's cake reaches Big Mom, halts her Hunger-Pang rampage ("die right now"), Perospero's broadcast credits Pudding & the disgraced Chiffon / Oven's Hot Sea Hell boils the sea; the Queen Mama Chanter nears Cacao; Big Mom's birthday-banner flashback)

## Whole Cake Island Arc (WCI 39 [901-902] — "One Last Thing" cont. / escape from Totto Land: Perospero rides Golden Tree Frog to Cacao to steal Luffy's soul; the Sun Pirates (Wadatsumi swallows the Sunny to hide it, "Wadakichi") sacrifice themselves so Jinbe can escort Luffy out — Aladine & crew hold the rear vs Oven's Hot-Sea-Torpedoes/Heat-Cross; Jinbe uses Fishman-Karate current-manipulation to sweep the Sunny clear, promises to meet in Wano; Stussy & the pursuit fleet let them go (Worst-Generation "next Pirate King" speech); Sanji mourns Pedro (Mokomo flashback), Cotton-Candy-Flurry border; Pudding-flashback (Big-Mom-puppet / third-eye / "creepy" bullying / Sanji "beautiful eye" / her "one last favour"); Mont d'Or's Territorial-Sea-Slug confirms escape, Germa 66 retreat; Katakuri & Brûlée mirror scene (his exposed mouth / pelican-eel childhood flashback / Brûlée scarred → he vows to teach true fear); Big Mom's "life or death" arrival; coda in East Blue — Baratie, Zeff feeds hungry criminals)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 08 [918-919] — "The Ruins of Oden Castle": Bakura Town escape — Luffy/Zoro flee with the provisions treasure ship; Otama tames Headliner Speed with a Millet Dumpling, Speed becomes their servant ("Master"); Luffy's Red Hawk / meanwhile Law (disguised, ROOM/Shambles) fights & unmasks before Basil Hawkins, who now knows the Luffy-Law alliance & that Holdem was beaten (reported to Jack→Kaido) / crew distribute the food to starving Okobore Town as "Luffytaro"; Law scolds Luffy for blowing cover / everyone heads to the ruins of Oden Castle atop the mountain; Law shows Luffy the graves of Oden/Momonosuke/Kanjuro/Raizo/Kin'emon & warns of "the ghosts of Wano" / Kin'emon (alive, had the runs), Kiku, Momonosuke reunite with the crew (Nami/Sanji/Brook + Bepo) / Kin'emon's bombshell: he, Kiku, Kanjuro, Raizo & Momonosuke are time-travellers from 20 years in the past)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 09 [919-920] — "I Love Oden": a Flower Capital classroom teaches the Orochi-approved (false) history — Oden & the Nine Red Scabbards branded villains, Shogun Kurozumi Orochi the "hero"; the rokkurokubi teacher; drunk yakuza boss "Napping" Kyoshiro badmouths Orochi (Robin/? eavesdrops for intel). Then Kin'emon's long narration to the crew: Kozuki Oden's legend (banished from the Flower Capital → tamed lawless Kuri, beat Ashura Doji, became daimyo, built the town/Halcyon Farm/docks; fascinated Whitebeard & earned Roger's respect) / Oden Castle (Kuri Castle) burned by Kaido/Orochi 20 yrs ago; Lady Kozuki Toki (time-traveller, Toki-Toki Fruit) sends Momonosuke, Hiyori? & the Scabbards to the future via her power, reciting her prophecy poem; Jibuemon the Kibi retainer greets them / the Scabbards scatter across the seas (Raizo→Zou, Momo/Kanjuro→Dressrosa/Doflamingo, Kin'emon→Punk Hazard/Caesar/chopped by Law) then reunite with the Straw Hats; the alliance roster & plan to behead Kaido & Orochi's leaders)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 10 [921-922] — "Shutenmaru": Oden Castle ruins — Kin'emon lays out the raid plan: strike on the Fire Festival day at Habu Port, Onigashima raid to behead Emperor Kaido & Shogun Kurozumi Orochi; assigns crew disguises via his Garb-Garb Technique (Nami→kunoichi Onami, Robin/Orobi→geisha, Franky/Franosuke→carpenter, Usopp/Usohachi & Zoro/Zorojuro→capital, Brook, Sanji cooking, Carrot+Chopper→Inuarashi); summons veteran kunoichi Shinobu (Seduction Technique) — Sanji's dismay / three key samurai to recruit: Kawamatsu, Ashura Doji, Denjiro (all Oden retainers) / scene shift to Okobore Town: Beasts thugs rob the poor; Boss Shutenmaru (=Ashura Doji, Mount Atama Thieves) arrives, Calamity Jack tries to recruit him for Kaido, they fight / Kaido himself descends as a dragon; Law ROOM/Shambles-grabs Luffy to stop him exposing the alliance; Kin'emon rushes to save his wife Tsuru; Hawkins lies to Kaido that Luffy/Law hide in Oden Castle; Kaido unleashes Blast Breath)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 11 [922-923] — "Emperor Kaido vs. Luffy": Okobore Town — a drunk dragon-form Kaido crash-lands after Blast Breath, flattening houses (villagers cry for parents); Shutenmaru's thieves flee up the mountain / Luffy Gear-Third Elephant-Guns the falling Kaido; townsfolk recognise the man who beat Urashima & Holdem / Kin'emon & Kiku reunite with Momonosuke by the Oden Castle ruins; Kin'emon, torn, rushes to Okobore Town to save his wife Tsuru while Kiku guards Momonosuke / Beasts Pirates find Headliner Speed fallen from Kaido — she failed to protect her "master" Otama, whom Viceroy Kaido seized en route home; Luffy/Otama's cutesy name "Horsella" / Luffy, over Law's objection, provokes Kaido directly ("I'm gonna be King of the Pirates!"); Kaido Blast-Breaths, "So you've come to Wano!" / Luffy's Gear-Fourth Boundman barrage — Elephant Gatling, Grizzly Magnum, Kong Gun, Double Culverin, Kong Organ, King Kong Gun — Kaido counters with Thunder Bagua; Luffy still breathing/glaring, Kaido mocks "What kind of king did you say you'd be?")

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 12 [924] — "Hah" (end of Act 1): opening — Basil Hawkins corners the escaping Trafalgar Law, robs his Room/Shambles strength with a seastone nail forged by Wano artisans, cuts with Straw Sword; Law flees via Shambles / young Eustass Kid, already captured, unleashes conqueror's haki while unconscious (the guards note it's "Lord Oden's technique") — Kaido leaves both for later; Hawkins reports Straw Hat caught, vows to hunt Law / Okobore Town aftermath of Kaido's Blast Breath: injured Mr. Gorobe treated, townsfolk worry Luffytaro blames himself; Ashura Doji / the sinkhole rescue — Shinobu ("Shino") rots the ground with her Ripe-Ripe Seduction Technique to save the women (Chopper Guard Point, Kaido Blast Breath in the flashback); the crew flee to the forest, Kaido harmed children / news of Luffytaro's Kuri fight spreads across Wano via notice papers; Flower Capital blacksmiths forced to forge for Shogun Orochi / scene shift to the Udon region prison mines — a mysterious poison-fish-eating prisoner in a cell; Luffy hauled in as a "heinous criminal", mocked as weak by guards, glares defiantly; a fishbone flies from the cell / episode ends "Rest up today — tomorrow's gonna be hell!" with Kid's brush-scrawl defiance)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 13 [925-926] — "Void" (start of Act 2): cold-open flashback on Gloom Island (陰鬱島, ruins of the Muggy Kingdom) — Perona lives with Mihawk; reads the paper that Gecko Moria survived the Summit War, gleefully leaves to find him / Beehive (蜂巢, Pirate Island in the New World): Moria storms the Blackbeard Pirates' base hunting Absalom; Blackbeard's Titanic Captains — Avalo Pizarro "Corrupt King", Shiryu "of the Rain" (now invisible via Absalom's Clear-Clear Fruit), Catarina Devon "Maiden Hunter" (killed Absalom) — confront him; Blackbeard's off-screen taunt / news-recap of Reverie Day 4: Revolutionary Army commanders storm Mariejois to take back Kuma, clash with Admirals Ryokugyu & Fujitora; scene turns to Wano / Oden Castle ruins: the Nine Red Scabbards (Inuarashi, Kiku, etc.) confront Ashura Doji, who refuses to rejoin; Raizo reports Luffy located at the Udon prison mine / Otama recovers, learns Luffy ("Big Bro") was hauled to Udon; Udon narration / Flower Capital: Orochi & geisha weapons order; Shinobu & Onami(Nami) spy from the attic (Great Kite escape); Sangoro(Sanji)'s soba stand recruits samurai; the news-crier spreads legends of Kamazo "the Reaper" & the Witching Hour Boy, and the Kozuki crescent-moon mark; kendo/martial-arts banned under Orochi / Raizo brings Luffy word Otama is better / Beasts Pirates: King & Queen belittle Jack over Kuri's revenue)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 14 [926-927] — "Otoko the Kamuro": Kibi ruins gambling den — Tonoyasu ("Yasu the Sycophant") wins big; a silent swordsman (Zoro) busts the Kyoshiro-Family cheats with No-Sword Style Dragon Twister & One-Sword Style Dragon Quake; Tonoyasu latches onto him, points out the Flower Capital & his home Ebisu the Leftovers Town / Udon prison mine: guards abuse Old Man Hyo; two fully-recovered prisoners (Luffy & "Jaggy") out-haul everyone, eat all the food, get swallowed & beaten by Vice Warden/Headliner Dobon (Hippo SMILE) but wreck him / Flower Capital: Sangoro(Sanji)'s special-soba stand with Orobi(Robin) draws crowds; Kyoshiro-Family thugs (Suke, Kaku) knock over a girl's soba, Sanji & Franosuke(Franky) thrash them (Franosuke Iron Suplex); the girl is the kamuro Toko/"Otoko" (name-pun on 男/otoko), attendant to the oiran Komurasaki, off to the oiran's procession to Orochi Castle; Robin gets a castle summons / Udon: Raizo reports the seastone-cuff key is guarded in a seastone box; Caribou pesters "Boss" Luffy for dumplings (Narrator recaps Caribou's saga) / Kyoshiro plans to have Queen send an assassin; Orochi awaits Komurasaki)

Surface alias only — do NOT use canon 霜月康家/霜月 (alias-vs-canon pattern, cf Kyoshiro/Kamazo/Otama). "My name is Tonoyasu"→我叫多康 |

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 15 [928-930] — "Oiran Komurasaki Arrives": cold-open Orochi haggling with a Government/broker agent (post-Doflamingo/Joker) — demands a warship, then Vegapunk, boasting Kaido's backing / Flower Capital: the oiran Komurasaki's procession; a comic vignette-story of how she cons besotted men (old "Bingo" and others) out of houses/families/500-gold buyouts, then coldly discards them ("men are just dogs who fetch me money"); Boss Kyoshiro presides; the poor expelled from the city / Oden Castle ruins: Momonosuke tells Tama of his little sister Hiyori (would be 26, fate unconfirmed) / Flower Capital: Franosuke(Franky), apprenticed to legendary master carpenter Minatomo (as "Franko"), hunts the Onigashima/Kaido's-mansion blueprints — a running comic chain tracing the pawned blueprints through Kumagoro→landlord Kobe→geisha Kisegawa→Tokijiro→"the Camel"→a masked man from Kuri; reports to Kin'emon via transponder / Ashura Doji plot: Scabbards raid the factory & leave a Mount-Atama-Thieves calling card to goad the Beast Pirates into attacking him and reignite his will / Ebisu Town: Tonoyasu(Yasu) brings Zoro home; the impoverished-but-laughing townsfolk, the Witching Hour Boy's latest gift, "Ebisu grin"; Orochi's wasteful banquet / Udon prison: Caribou begs Luffy("Boss") to break out together; talk of "Jaggy"=Kid's reputation vs Big Mom; Kid's rebuttal (wounded a Sweet Commander, lost arm to Red Hair Pirates); Luffy & Kid both vow to topple Kaido / Fire-Festival eve; the Big Mom Pirates' carp-drawn ship storms the waterfall into Wano — Orochi's "I'll kill you, Linlin!"; Kaido "I won't let you get away, Straw Hat")

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 16 [930-931] — "Soba Mask": cold-open — Brook infiltrates a mysterious room (dolls, a poneglyph) via surveillance-camera feed / the Big Mom Pirates' carp-drawn ship climbs the falls into Wano — Beast Pirates panic, "Viceroy Kaido" & King (pteranodon form) shoots the ship down; Big Mom's brats swept into the sea / Flower Capital: Page One (Soaring Six, Beast Pirates) hunts "Sangoro the soba cook" (Sanji) for wrecking the Kyoshiro Family; the crew (Franosuke/Usohachi + townsfolk) flee, but Sanji refuses to let a soba shop be destroyed — reveals himself as "Sangoro of the Special Soba" (Diable Jambe: Concassé) then dons the Germa 66 Raid Suit becoming Stealth Black, renaming himself "Soba Mask"; Nami/Usopp recap Sora, Warrior of the Sea & the North-Blue Germa lore; invisibility (like the Clear-Clear Fruit) + Vinsmoke-brother flashback; beats Page One with Meteoric Soba Kick, flees toward Ebisu / meanwhile the Oniwabanshu (Fukurokuju, head) corner Robin (Orobi) searching for a poneglyph; kamuro & Otama(阿玉)+Chopperemon ninja-play (Ninpo techniques, Komurasaki's masked tune) / Zoro & co. stumble on a giant sleeping woman — the infamous Big Mom, waking with amnesia ("Who am I?!"))

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 17 [932-933] — "The Shogun and the Oiran": Orochi Castle attic — the Oniwabanshu (Fukurokuju) interrogate the captured "geisha" Orobi (Robin) as the Witching Hour Boy; she escapes via a clone, radios crew (Onami/Nami, Shinobu, Bonekichi/Brook) — 11 ninja spotted; Brook (as gashadokuro specter) hunts for Robin's real body / Orochi's banquet — Komurasaki(oiran)/Kyoshiro(Boss)/Otoko(kamuro) attend; Orochi rants about the Kozuki-Oden 20-yr vengeance prophecy (Toki's poem), the Nine Red Scabbards & Foxfire Kin'emon coming for his head; Otoko can't stop laughing (SMILE) & enrages Orochi — Komurasaki slaps him, refuses to grovel ("daughter of a samurai"); Orochi transforms (Yamata-no-Orochi 8-headed serpent) to devour her, but Kyoshiro cuts Komurasaki down "as a samurai's mercy"; kamuro grief / Brook/Robin extract the crew, Shinobu vs old flame Hanzo (Ninpo: Ballbuster/Paralysis/Rot Garden), Onami's Thunderbolt escape / Ebisu Town: Otama/Chopperemon & Otsuru feed oshiruko to the amnesiac giant "Olin" (Big Mom); plot to send her to Udon (lie: the Superstar there loves oshiruko) to rescue "Big Bro" Luffy)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 18 [934-935] — "Hyogoro the Flower": Big Mom Pirates stranded at Kuri waterfall base — Perospero declares himself captain (lick-puns), crew squabbles / Otama-Kiku-Momonosuke boat to Udon with amnesiac "Olin" (Big Mom) & the sharkodile; SNATCH rallying-cry gag (Zoro-taught, Kiku forbids it as vulgar old-Kuri battle cry) / Kin'emon-Kanjuro-Shinobu-Orobi(Robin)-Bonekichi(Brook) regroup in Ringo — Komurasaki's murder by Napping Kyoshiro, Orobi's intel haul + a non-red poneglyph (lode poneglyph to Laugh Tale must be on Onigashima), plan a Flower Capital bathhouse / Nami-Sanji-Law(Traf) hideout learn Kid escaped Udon prison via newspaper / Udon Prison Mine: guards abuse Old Man Hyo (Hyogoro the Flower), Luffytaro(Luffy) defiant & eats Hyo's confiscated millet dumplings; Raizo steals a seastone-cuff key (afterimage ninjutsu); Warden Babanuki (Elephant SMILE, Elephant Achoo) vs Luffy as Superstar Queen arrives fawning over dead Komurasaki / bathhouse: Sanji sneaks in (mixed bath), Shinobu's Ninpo Ball Buster, Sansuke the octopus back-washer, more Hyogoro-legend exposition / Queen recognizes starved Hyogoro & schemes; Ringo: Raizo reunites with Kawamatsu)

## Wano Country Arc (Wano 17 [932-933] — "The Shogun and the Oiran": Orochi Castle attic — the Oniwabanshu (Fukurokuju) interrogate the captured "geisha" Orobi (Robin) as the Witching Hour Boy; she escapes via a clone, radios crew (Onami/Nami, Shinobu, Bonekichi/Brook) — 11 ninja spotted; Brook (as gashadokuro specter) hunts for Robin's real body / Orochi's banquet — Komurasaki(oiran)/Kyoshiro(Boss)/Otoko(kamuro) attend; Orochi rants about the Kozuki-Oden 20-yr vengeance prophecy (Toki's poem), the Nine Red Scabbards & Foxfire Kin'emon coming for his head; Otoko can't stop laughing (SMILE) & enrages Orochi — Komurasaki slaps him, refuses to grovel ("daughter of a samurai"); Orochi transforms (Yamata-no-Orochi 8-headed serpent) to devour her, but Kyoshiro cuts Komurasaki down "as a samurai's mercy"; kamuro grief / Brook/Robin extract the crew, Shinobu vs old flame Hanzo (Ninpo: Ballbuster/Paralysis/Rot Garden), Onami's Thunderbolt escape / Ebisu Town: Otama/Chopperemon & Otsuru feed oshiruko to the amnesiac giant "Olin" (Big Mom); plot to send her to Udon (lie: the Superstar there loves oshiruko) to rescue "Big Bro" Luffy)
